Probes what each mode can do (Bitable / Drive / Docs), resolves the effective read mode (preference user(default)|auto|tenant; user prefers the real-person token so writes are traceable, auto picks the mode with more granted read scopes tie→tenant), writes structured lark info to lark-auth.state.json + env, and reports any conflict with what a command needs.
